====================================================================================================
QUESTION: What happens after 2 years if the policy owner stops paying premiums?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The policy automatically continues with a reduced benefit amount based on the cash value; no further premiums are needed.
EVIDENCE: 8.1 After 2 years, if you stop paying premiums, your policy will automatically continue with a reduced benefit amount based on the cash value. No further premiums needed.
====================================================================================================
